Basic STA/LTA picker\n                window = int(0.05 * fs)  # 50ms window\n                threshold = 5.0\n                trigger_times = []\n\n                # Simple peak detection above threshold\n                threshold_val = np.std(data) * threshold\n                above_thresh = np.where(np.abs(data) > threshold_val)[0]\n\n                if len(above_thresh) > 0:\n                    # Group nearby peaks\n                    groups = np.split(above_thresh, np.where(np.diff(above_thresh) > fs)[0] + 1)\n\n                    for group in groups:\n                        if len(group) > window:\n                            peak_idx = group[np.argmax(np.abs(data[group]))]\n                            trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx / fs\n\n                            # Update event time as median of picks\n                event_time = event_time\n            else:\n                # Process current event if it has enough picks\n                if len(current_event) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 stations\n                    # Calculate event time as median of P-picks\n                    p_picks = [p for p in current_event if p['phase'] == 'P']\n                    if p_picks:\n                        event_times = [p['time'].timestamp for p in p_picks]\n                        median_time = np.median(event_times)\n                        associated_events.append({\n                            'time': UTCDateTime(median_time).isoformat()\n                        })\n\n                # It uses multivariate Gaussian distribution to model the collection of phase picks of an event, and uses Expectation-Maximization to carry out pick assignment and estimate source parameters i.e., earthquake location, origin time, and magnitude.\n\n`GaMMA` is a python library implementing the algorithm. For the input earthquake traces, this library assumes P/S wave picks have already been extracted. We provide documentation of its core API.\n\n`Zhu, W., McBrearty, I. W., Mousavi, S. M., Ellsworth, W. L., & Beroza, G. C. (2022). Earthquake phase association using a Bayesian Gaussian mixture model. Journal of Geophysical Research: Solid Earth, 127(5).`\n\nThe skill is a derivative of the repo https://github.com/AI4EPS/GaMMA\n\n## Installing GaMMA\n`pip install git+https://github.com/wayneweiqiang/GaMMA.git`\n\n## GaMMA core API\n\n\n### `association`\n\n#### Function Signature\n\n```python\ndef association(picks, stations, config, event_idx0=0, method=\"BGMM\", **kwargs)\n```\n\n#### Purpose\n\nAssociates seismic phase picks (P and S waves) to earthquake events using Bayesian or standard Gaussian Mixture Models. It clusters picks based on arrival time and amplitude information, then fits GMMs to estimate earthquake locations, times, and magnitudes.\n\n#### 1. Configure GaMMA\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 5,\n        'max_sigma11': 2.0,\n        'use_amplitude': True,\n        'max_sigma22': 1.0,\n        'max_sigma12': 1.0,\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 3,\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using simple energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Filter trace\n            data = trace.data.copy()\n            fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Simple energy-based picker\n            # Calculate moving average\n            window = int(0.5 * fs)  # 0.5 second window\n            threshold = np.std(data) * 10\n\n            # Find peaks above threshold\n            from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n            peaks, properties = find_peaks(np.abs(data), height=threshold, distance=fs)\n\n            for peak in peaks:\n                trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak / fs\n                picks_list.append({\n                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                    'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                    'type': 'p',  # Assume P-wave\n                    'prob': 0.7,\n                    'amp': np.abs(data[peak])\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error in fallback picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'])\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 5,\n        'max_sigma11': 2.0,\n        'use_amplitude': True,\n        'max_sigma22': 1.0,\n        'max_sigma12': 1.0,\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 3,\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Add DBSCAN parameters\n    config['use_dbscan'] = True\n    config['dbscan_eps'] = 15.0  # seconds\n    config['dbscan_min_samples'] = 3\n    config['dbscan_min_cluster_size'] = 500\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 10\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading data...\")\n    waveform_stream = load_waveform_data(mseed_path)\n    station_df, unique_stations = load_station_data(station_path)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n2. Picking phases...\")\n    picks = pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    if len(picks) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No picks generated!\")\n        return\n\n    # Prepare data for GaMMA\n    print(\"\\n3. Preparing data for GaMMA...\")\n    gamma_stations_df = prepare_stations_for_gamma(station_df)\n    picks_df = prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    print(\"\\n4. Associating phases...\")\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n5. Writing results...\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        results = []\n        for event in events:\n            results.append({\n                'time': event['time']\n            })\n\n        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n        results_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n        print(f\"SUCCESS: Written {len(results)} events to {output_path}\")\n        print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n        print(results_df.head())\n    else:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"WARNING: No events found, created empty results file\")\n\n    print(\"\\nPhase association complete!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":6,"newStart":1,"newLines":7,"lines":[" #!/usr/bin/env python3"," \"\"\""," Seismic phase association script for earthquake detection.","+Uses SeisBench for phase picking and GaMMA for association."," \"\"\""," "," import pandas as pd"]},{"oldStart":9,"oldLines":18,"newStart":10,"newLines":25,"lines":[" import warnings"," warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')"," ","+# Obspy imports","+from obspy import read","+from obspy.core import UTCDateTime","+import obspy.signal.filter","+"," # Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using simple energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Filter trace\n            data = trace.data.copy()\n            fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Simple energy-based picker\n            # Calculate moving average\n            window = int(0.5 * fs)  # 0.5 second window\n            threshold = np.std(data) * 10\n\n            # Find peaks above threshold\n            from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n            peaks, properties = find_peaks(np.abs(data), height=threshold, distance=fs)\n\n            for peak in peaks:\n                trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak / fs\n                picks_list.append({\n                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                    'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                    'type': 'p',  # Assume P-wave\n                    'prob': 0.7,\n                    'amp': np.abs(data[peak])\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error in fallback picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'])\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 5,\n        'max_sigma11': 2.0,\n        'use_amplitude': True,\n        'max_sigma22': 1.0,\n        'max_sigma12': 1.0,\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 3,\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Add DBSCAN parameters\n    config['use_dbscan'] = True\n    config['dbscan_eps'] = 15.0  # seconds\n    config['dbscan_min_samples'] = 3\n    config['dbscan_min_cluster_size'] = 500\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 10\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Associating phases...\")\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n5. Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Filter trace\n            data = trace.data.copy()\n            fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Simple energy-based picker\n            # Calculate moving average\n            window = int(0.5 * fs)  # 0.5 second window\n            threshold = np.std(data) * 10\n\n            # Find peaks above threshold\n            from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n            peaks, properties = find_peaks(np.abs(data), height=threshold, distance=fs)\n\n            for peak in peaks:\n                trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak / fs\n                picks_list.append({\n                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                    'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                    'type': 'p',  # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1027,"cache_read_input_tokens":42633},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36ffb446-6489-40e9-9a00-881ae1f382e6","uuid":"3af935cc-8ea0-45a3-bc7d-6785bf11c212"}

Increased from 1.0\n        'max_sigma12': 2.0,  # Increased from 1.0\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 2,  # Reduced from 3\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Reduced from 500\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 20  # Increased from 10","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic phase association script for earthquake detection.\nUses SeisBench for phase picking and GaMMA for association.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Obspy imports\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy.core import UTCDateTime\nimport obspy.signal.filter\n\n# SeisBench imports\nimport seisbench.data as sbdata\nimport seisbench.models as sbmodels\n\n# GaMMA imports\nfrom gamma.utils import association, estimate_eps\n\ndef load_waveform_data(mseed_path):\n    \"\"\"Load waveform data from MSEED file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading waveform data from {mseed_path}...\")\n    st = read(mseed_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n    print(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n    return st\n\ndef load_station_data(csv_path):\n    \"\"\"Load station information from CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading station data from {csv_path}...\")\n    stations = pd.read_csv(csv_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations)} station channels\")\n\n    # Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format - handle mixed formats\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 5,\n        'max_sigma11': 2.0,\n        'use_amplitude': True,\n        'max_sigma22': 1.0,\n        'max_sigma12': 1.0,\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 3,\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Add DBSCAN parameters\n    config['use_dbscan'] = True\n    config['dbscan_eps'] = 15.0  # seconds\n    config['dbscan_min_samples'] = 3\n    config['dbscan_min_cluster_size'] = 500\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 10\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading data...\")\n    waveform_stream = load_waveform_data(mseed_path)\n    station_df, unique_stations = load_station_data(station_path)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n2. Picking phases...\")\n    picks = pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    if len(picks) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No picks generated!\")\n        return\n\n    # Prepare data for GaMMA\n    print(\"\\n3. Preparing data for GaMMA...\")\n    gamma_stations_df = prepare_stations_for_gamma(station_df)\n    picks_df = prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    print(\"\\n4. Associating phases...\")\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n5. Writing results...\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        results = []\n        for event in events:\n            results.append({\n                'time': event['time']\n            })\n\n        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n        results_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n        print(f\"SUCCESS: Written {len(results)} events to {output_path}\")\n        print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n        print(results_df.head())\n    else:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"WARNING: No events found, created empty results file\")\n\n    print(\"\\nPhase association complete!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":342,"oldLines":17,"newStart":342,"newLines":17,"lines":["         print(\"No picks available for association!\")","         return []"," ","-    # Configure GaMMA","+    # Configure GaMMA - Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Increased from 1.0\n        'max_sigma12': 2.0,  # Increased from 1.0\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 2,  # Reduced from 3\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Reduced from 500\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 20  # Increased from 10\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Configure GaMMA - very lenient settings\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 2,  # Very lenient\n        'max_sigma11': 5.0,  # Very lenient\n        'use_amplitude': False,  # Disable to simplify\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 1,  # Very lenient\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Try without DBSCAN first\n    config['use_dbscan'] = False\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method, no DBSCAN)...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        #
