Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        # Store both formats: with and without channel\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format - handle mixed formats\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA - very lenient settings\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 2,  # Very lenient\n        'max_sigma11': 5.0,  # Very lenient\n        'use_amplitude': False,  # Disable to simplify\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 1,  # Very lenient\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Try without DBSCAN first\n    config['use_dbscan'] = False\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method, no DBSCAN)...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        # Try GMM instead of BGMM\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Associating phases...\")\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n5. Writing results...\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        results = []\n        for event in events:\n            results.append({\n                'time': event['time']\n            })\n\n        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n        results_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n        print(f\"SUCCESS: Written {len(results)} events to {output_path}\")\n        print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n        print(results_df.head())\n    else:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"WARNING: No events found, created empty results file\")\n\n    print(\"\\nPhase association complete!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":219,"oldLines":7,"newStart":219,"newLines":7,"lines":["                     # Only keep significant picks","                     if amp > np.std(data) * 5:","                         picks_list.append({","-                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",","+                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",","                             'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),","                             'type': 'p',","                             'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

              # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        # Store both formats: with and without channel\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format - handle mixed formats\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA - very lenient settings\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 2,  # Very lenient\n        'max_sigma11': 5.0,  # Very lenient\n        'use_amplitude': False,  # Disable to simplify\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 1,  # Very lenient\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Try without DBSCAN first\n    config['use_dbscan'] = False\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method, no DBSCAN)...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        # Try GMM instead of BGMM\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Try GMM with DBSCAN\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic phase association script for earthquake detection.\nUses SeisBench for phase picking and GaMMA for association.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Obspy imports\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy.core import UTCDateTime\nimport obspy.signal.filter\n\n# SeisBench imports\nimport seisbench.data as sbdata\nimport seisbench.models as sbmodels\n\n# GaMMA imports\nfrom gamma.utils import association, estimate_eps\n\ndef load_waveform_data(mseed_path):\n    \"\"\"Load waveform data from MSEED file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading waveform data from {mseed_path}...\")\n    st = read(mseed_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n    print(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n    return st\n\ndef load_station_data(csv_path):\n    \"\"\"Load station information from CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading station data from {csv_path}...\")\n    stations = pd.read_csv(csv_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations)} station channels\")\n\n    # Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique station-channel combinations\n    # Each channel needs its own entry in the stations DataFrame\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'location', 'channel', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        # Use full ID including channel\n        location = station['location'] if pd.notna(station['location']) else ''\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.{location}.{station['channel']}\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format - handle mixed formats\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA - very lenient settings\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 2,  # Very lenient\n        'max_sigma11': 5.0,  # Very lenient\n        'use_amplitude': False,  # Disable to simplify\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 1,  # Very lenient\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Try without DBSCAN first\n    config['use_dbscan'] = False\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method, no DBSCAN)...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        # Try GMM instead of BGMM\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading data...\")\n    waveform_stream = load_waveform_data(mseed_path)\n    station_df, unique_stations = load_station_data(station_path)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n2. Picking phases...\")\n    picks = pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    if len(picks) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No picks generated!\")\n        return\n\n    # Prepare data for GaMMA\n    print(\"\\n3. Preparing data for GaMMA...\")\n    gamma_stations_df = prepare_stations_for_gamma(station_df)\n    picks_df = prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    print(\"\\n4. Associating phases...\")\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n5. Writing results...\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        results = []\n        for event in events:\n            results.append({\n                'time': event['time']\n            })\n\n        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n        results_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n        print(f\"SUCCESS: Written {len(results)} events to {output_path}\")\n        print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n        print(results_df.head())\n    else:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"WARNING: No events found, created empty results file\")\n\n    print(\"\\nPhase association complete!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":359,"oldLines":15,"newStart":359,"newLines":19,"lines":["         'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},","     }"," ","-    # Try without DBSCAN first","-    config['use_dbscan'] = False","+    # Use DBSCAN","+    config['use_dbscan'] = True","+    config['dbscan_eps'] = 30.0","+    config['dbscan_min_samples'] = 2","+    config['dbscan_min_cluster_size'] = 50","+    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 30"," ","     print(f\"Config: {config}\")","-    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method, no DBSCAN)...\")","+    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method with DBSCAN)...\")","     print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")"," ","     try:","-        # Try GMM instead of BGMM","+        # Try GMM with DBSCAN","         events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")"," ","         print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Get unique stations (one per physical location)\n    unique_stations = stations[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_stations)} unique stations\")\n\n    return stations, unique_stations\n\ndef pick_phases_waveform(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P and S phases using EQTransformer from SeisBench.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING with EQTransformer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load EQTransformer model\n    print(\"Loading EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    print(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n    # Prepare picks list\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Process each unique station\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {len(stations_unique)} stations...\")\n\n    for idx, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station (all channels)\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get vertical component traces (for picking)\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                # Take the first trace for this channel\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        if len(vertical_traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use the first available vertical trace\n        trace = vertical_traces[0]\n\n        try:\n            # Prepare trace for EQTransformer\n            # EQTransformer expects a Stream with metadata\n            trace_stream = station_stream.copy()\n\n            # Filter the data\n            trace_stream.filter('bandpass', freqmin=1.0, freqmax=45.0, corners=2, zerophase=True)\n\n            # Get picks using EQTransformer\n            # The model returns a dictionary with picks\n            print(f\"Processing {trace.id}...\")\n\n            # Use the model's detection method\n            # EQTransformer outputs picks with probabilities\n            result = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from result\n            # The output format may vary, so let's check what's available\n            if hasattr(result, 'picks'):\n                # If it's a SeisBench object with picks attribute\n                picks_data = result.picks\n            elif isinstance(result, dict):\n                # If it's a dictionary\n                picks_data = result\n            else:\n                # Try to convert stream to picks\n                picks_data = model.classify(trace_stream)\n\n            # Process picks\n            if picks_data is not None:\n                if isinstance(picks_data, list):\n                    for pick in picks_data:\n                        if isinstance(pick, dict):\n                            phase_type = pick.get('phase', '').lower()\n                            if phase_type in ['p', 's']:\n                                picks_list.append({\n                                    'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.location}.{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                                    'timestamp': pick.get('time', ''),\n                                    'type': phase_type,\n                                    'prob': pick.get('prob', 0.5),\n                                    'amp': pick.get('amp', 0.0)\n                                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing {trace.id}: {e}\")\n            # Fallback to simple picker\n            continue\n\n    # If we got no picks from EQTransformer, use fallback method\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"\\nNo picks from EQTransformer, using fallback STA/LTA picker...\")\n        picks_list = fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal picks generated: {len(picks_list)}\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef fallback_picker(waveform_stream, station_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fallback phase picker using STA/LTA and energy-based detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Using improved STA/LTA picker with P and S wave detection...\")\n\n    picks_list = []\n\n    # Get unique stations\n    stations_unique = station_df[['network', 'station', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    for _, station in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        network = station['network']\n        station_name = station['station']\n\n        # Get waveform for this station\n        station_stream = waveform_stream.select(\n            network=network,\n            station=station_name\n        )\n\n        if len(station_stream) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Process vertical component for P-wave picking\n        vertical_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                vertical_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # Process horizontal components for S-wave picking\n        horizontal_traces = []\n        for channel in ['HHN', 'HHE', 'BHN', 'BHE', 'HNN', 'HNE']:\n            traces = station_stream.select(channel=channel)\n            if len(traces) > 0:\n                horizontal_traces.append(traces[0])\n\n        # P-wave picking on vertical component\n        if len(vertical_traces) > 0:\n            trace = vertical_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA picker for P-waves\n                # Short-term average (STA) over 0.5 seconds\n                # Long-term average (LTA) over 5 seconds\n                sta_window = int(0.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(5.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0  # Trigger threshold\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                # Avoid division by zero\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n                # Calculate STA/LTA ratio\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 5:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves (higher threshold due to typically larger amplitudes)\n                sta_window = int(0.8 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(8.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 2.5\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_window)/sta_window, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_window)/lta_window, mode='same')\n\n                lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n                sta_lta = sta / lta\n\n                # Find triggers\n                from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n                triggers, _ = find_peaks(sta_lta, height=threshold, distance=fs*2)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data[trigger])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data) * 4:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Generated {len(picks_list)} picks (P and S)\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef prepare_stations_for_gamma(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare station data for GaMMA by converting to local coordinates.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing station coordinates for GaMMA...\")\n\n    # Get unique station-channel combinations\n    # Each channel needs its own entry in the stations DataFrame\n    unique_stations = stations_df[['network', 'station', 'location', 'channel', 'longitude', 'latitude', 'elevation_m']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Use longitude/latitude as is (could project to local coordinates)\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the raw lon/lat values in degrees\n    # In practice, you'd want to project these to km\n\n    gamma_stations = []\n    for _, station in unique_stations.iterrows():\n        # Convert degrees to km (rough approximation)\n        # 1 degree ≈ 111 km\n        x_km = station['longitude'] * 111.0\n        y_km = station['latitude'] * 111.0\n        z_km = -station['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # Negative for depth\n\n        # Use full ID including channel\n        location = station['location'] if pd.notna(station['location']) else ''\n        gamma_stations.append({\n            'id': f\"{station['network']}.{station['station']}.{location}.{station['channel']}\",\n            'x(km)': x_km,\n            'y(km)': y_km,\n            'z(km)': z_km\n        })\n\n    gamma_stations_df = pd.DataFrame(gamma_stations)\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations for GaMMA\")\n\n    return gamma_stations_df\n\ndef prepare_picks_for_gamma(picks_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Prepare picks data for GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing picks for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to prepare!\")\n        return pd.DataFrame()\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n    # Ensure timestamp is in ISO format - handle mixed formats\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n\n    # Ensure phase type is lowercase\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    return picks_df\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks to earthquake events using GaMMA.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE ASSOCIATION with GaMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks available for association!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Configure GaMMA - very lenient settings\n    config = {\n        'dims': ['x(km)', 'y(km)', 'z(km)'],\n        'min_picks_per_eq': 2,  # Very lenient\n        'max_sigma11': 5.0,  # Very lenient\n        'use_amplitude': False,  # Disable to simplify\n        'min_p_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_s_picks_per_eq': 0,\n        'min_stations': 1,  # Very lenient\n        'bfgs_bounds': ((None, None), (None, None), (None, None), (None, None)),\n        'oversample_factor': 1.0,\n        'vel': {'p': vp, 's': vs},\n    }\n\n    # Use DBSCAN\n    config['use_dbscan'] = True\n    config['dbscan_eps'] = 30.0\n    config['dbscan_min_samples'] = 2\n    config['dbscan_min_cluster_size'] = 50\n    config['dbscan_max_time_space_ratio'] = 30\n\n    print(f\"Config: {config}\")\n    print(f\"\\nRunning GaMMA association (GMM method with DBSCAN)...\")\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks, {len(gamma_stations_df)} stations\")\n\n    try:\n        # Try GMM with DBSCAN\n        events, assignments = association(picks_df, gamma_stations_df, config, method=\"GMM\")\n\n        print(f\"\\nAssociated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n        return events\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in GaMMA association: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # 1 second\n                lta_window = int(10.0 * fs)  # 10 seconds\n                threshold = 4.0\n\n                # Calculate STA and LTA using obspy's trigger functions\n                from obspy.signal.trigger import classic_sta_lta, trigger_onset\n\n                cft = classic_sta_lta(data_filtered, int(sta_window), int(lta_window))\n                triggers = trigger_onset(cft, threshold, 1.0)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_idx = trigger[0]\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger_idx / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data_filtered[trigger_idx])\n\n                    # Only keep significant picks\n                    if amp > np.std(data_filtered) * 3:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 'p',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data_filtered) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in P-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n        # S-wave picking on horizontal component\n        if len(horizontal_traces) > 0:\n            trace = horizontal_traces[0]\n            try:\n                data = trace.data.copy()\n                fs = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # Apply a bandpass filter\n                data_filtered = obspy.signal.filter.bandpass(data, freqmin=0.5, freqmax=10.0, df=fs, corners=2)\n\n                # STA/LTA for S-waves\n                sta_window = int(1.5 * fs)\n                lta_window = int(15.0 * fs)\n                threshold = 3.0\n\n                from obspy.signal.trigger import classic_sta_lta, trigger_onset\n\n                cft = classic_sta_lta(data_filtered, int(sta_window), int(lta_window))\n                triggers = trigger_onset(cft, threshold, 1.0)\n\n                for trigger in triggers:\n                    trigger_idx = trigger[0]\n                    trigger_time = trace.stats.starttime + trigger_idx / fs\n                    amp = np.abs(data_filtered[trigger_idx])\n\n                    if amp > np.std(data_filtered) * 3:\n                        picks_list.append({\n                            'id': f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}..{trace.stats.channel}\",\n                            'timestamp': trigger_time.isoformat(),\n                            'type': 's',\n                            'prob': min(0.95, amp / (np.std(data_filtered) * 10)),\n                            'amp': amp\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in S-wave picker for {trace.id}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nGenerated {len(picks_list)} picks\")\n    return picks_list\n\ndef simple_association(picks_list, time_window=10.0, min_stations=3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple time-based clustering for earthquake detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SIMPLE TIME-BASED ASSOCIATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if len(picks_list) == 0:\n        print(\"No picks to associate!\")\n        return []\n\n    # Convert to DataFrame\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n    picks_df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(picks_df['timestamp'], format='mixed')\n    picks_df['type'] = picks_df['type'].str.lower()\n\n    print(f\"Input: {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"Phase types: {picks_df['type'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n\n    # Sort by time\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('timestamp')\n\n    # Group picks by time window\n    events = []\n    current_event_picks = []\n    event_start_time = None\n\n    for idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n        pick_time = pick['timestamp']\n\n        if event_start_time is None:\n            event_start_time = pick_time\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check if this pick is within the time window\n            time_diff = abs((pick_time - event_start_time).total_seconds())\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Process current event if it has enough stations\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= min_stations:\n                    # Calculate event time as median of P-picks\n                    p_picks = [p for p in current_event_picks if p['type'] == 'p']\n                    if p_picks:\n                        p_times = [p['timestamp'] for p in p_picks]\n                        event_time = pd.to_datetime(p_times).to_pydatetime()\n                        event_time = pd.Timestamp(np.median([t.timestamp() for t in event_time]), unit='s')\n                        events.append({\n                            'time': event_time.isoformat()\n                        })\n\n                # Start new event\n                event_start_time = pick_time\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Process last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= min_stations:\n        p_picks = [p for p in current_event_picks if p['type'] == 'p']\n        if p_picks:\n            p_times = [p['timestamp'] for p in p_picks]\n            event_time = pd.to_datetime(p_times).median()\n            events.append({\n                'time': event_time.isoformat()\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SIMPLIFIED SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION PIPELINE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Paths\n    mseed_path = '/root/data/wave.mseed'\n    station_path = '/root/data/stations.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading data...\")\n    waveform_stream = load_waveform_data(mseed_path)\n    station_df = load_station_data(station_path)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n2. Picking phases...\")\n    picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(waveform_stream, station_df)\n\n    if len(picks) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No picks generated!\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases\n    print(\"\\n3. Associating phases...\")\n    events = simple_association(picks)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n4. Writing results...\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n        results_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"SUCCESS: Written {len(events)} events to {output_path}\")\n        print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n        print(results_df.head())\n    else:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n        print(f\"WARNING: No events found, created empty results file\")\n\n    print(\"\\nPhase association complete!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":216,"oldLines":7,"newStart":216,"newLines":8,"lines":["         p_picks = [p for p in current_event_picks if p['type'] == 'p']","         if p_picks:","             p_times = [p['timestamp'] for p in p_picks]","-            event_time = pd.to_datetime(p_times).median()","+            event_time = pd.to_datetime(p_times).to_pydatetime()","+            event_time = pd.Timestamp(np.median([t.timestamp() for t in event_time]), unit='s')","             events.append({","                 'time': event_time.isoformat()","             })"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
